def user_lookup_uses_provided_email(trajectory, current_step_index):
    """Require an email lookup to use the email stated by the user."""
    import json
    import re
    print('user_lookup_uses_provided_email')
    steps = trajectory['steps']
    calls = []
    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
    for sub in steps[current_step_index].get('substeps', []):
        if sub.get('role') != 'assistant':
            continue
        text = sub.get('content', '')
        if not isinstance(text, str):
            raise TypeError('assistant content must be a string')
        for pos, char in enumerate(text):
            if char not in '[{':
                continue
            try:
                value, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[pos:])
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                continue
            for item in (value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]):
                if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get('function'), dict) and item['function'].get('name') == 'find_user_id_by_email':
                    raw_args = item['function'].get('arguments', '{}')
                    args = json.loads(raw_args) if isinstance(raw_args, str) else raw_args
               …(trunc)

      "def name_zip_lookup_matches_user_details(trajectory, current_step_index):",
      "    \"\"\"Require a name-and-ZIP lookup to use the user's stated details.\"\"\"",
      "    import json",
      "    import re",
      "    print('name_zip_lookup_matches_user_details')",
      "    steps = trajectory['steps']",
      "    calls = []",
      "    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()",
      "    for sub in steps[current_step_index].get('substeps', []):",
      "        if sub.get('role') != 'assistant':",
      "            continue",
      "        text = sub.get('content', '')",
      "        if not isinstance(text, str):",
      "            raise TypeError('assistant content must be a string')",
      "        for pos, char in enumerate(text):",
      "            if char not in '[{':",
      "                continue",
      "            try:",
      "                value, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[pos:])",
      "            except json.JSONDecodeError:",
      "                continue",
      "            for item in (value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]):",
      "                if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get('function'), dict) and item['function'].get('name') == 'find_user_id_by_name_zip':",
      "                    raw_args = item['function'].get('arguments', '{}')",
      "                    args = json.loads(raw_args) if isinstance(raw_args, str) else raw_args",
      "                    if not isinstance(args, dict):",
      "                        raise TypeError('tool arguments must be an object')",
      "                    calls.append(args)",
      "            if calls:",
      "                break",
      "    if not calls:",
      "        print('lookup_calls=', calls)",
      "        return True",
      "    prior_user_text = ' '.join(sub.get('content', '') for step in steps[:current_step_index] for sub in step.get('substeps', []) if sub.get('role') == 'user')",
      "    if not isinstance(prior_user_text, str):",
      "        raise TypeError('user content must be a string')",
      "    name_match = re.search(r\"name(?:'s|’s)\\s+([A-Za-z]+)\\s+([A-Za-z]+)\", prior_user_text, re.IGNORECASE)",
      "    zip_match = re.search(r'zip code is\\s*(\\d{5})', prior_user_text, re.IGNORECASE)",
      "    if not name_match or not zip_match:",
      "        print('name_match=', name_match, 'zip_match=', zip_match)",
      "        return False",
      "    expected = {'first_name': name_match.group(1), 'last_name': name_match.group(2), 'zip': zip_match.group(1)}",
      "    matches = all(args.get(key) == value for args in calls for key, value in expected.items())",
      "    print('lookup_calls=', calls, 'expected=', expected, 'matches=', matches)",
      "    if not matches:",
      "        return False",
      "    return True"
    ]
  },

      "                        args = json.loads(raw_args) if isinstance(raw_args, str) else raw_args",
      "                        if not isinstance(args, dict):",
      "                            raise TypeError('tool arguments must be an object')",
      "                        calls.append((fn.get('name'), args))",
      "                if calls:",
      "                    break",
      "        return calls",
      "    action_calls = calls_in(steps[action_step_index])",
      "    detail_calls = [args for name, args in action_calls if name == 'get_user_details']",
      "    if not detail_calls:",
      "        print('get_user_details_calls=', detail_calls)",
      "        return True",
      "    requested_id = detail_calls[-1].get('user_id')",
      "    lookup_result = None",
      "    for lookup_index in range(action_step_index - 1, -1, -1):",
      "        lookup_calls = calls_in(steps[lookup_index])",
      "        if not any(name in {'find_user_id_by_email', 'find_user_id_by_name_zip'} for name, _ in lookup_calls):",
      "            continue",
      "        for result_step in steps[lookup_index + 1:action_step_index]:",
      "            for sub in result_step.get('substeps', []):",
      "                if sub.get('role') == 'tool':",
      "                    content = sub.get('content', '')",
      "                    if not isinstance(content, str):",
      "                        raise TypeError('tool content must be a string')",
      "                    lookup_result = content.strip()",
      "                    break",
      "            if lookup_result is not None:",

def user_details_lookup_matches_authenticated_id(trajectory, current_step_index):
    """Ensure user details are retrieved for the ID returned by identity lookup."""
    import json
    print('user_details_lookup_matches_authenticated_id')
    steps = trajectory['steps']
    action_step_index = current_step_index - 1
    if action_step_index < 0:
        return True
    def calls_in(step):
        calls = []
        decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
        for sub in step.get('substeps', []):
            if sub.get('role') != 'assistant':
                continue
            text = sub.get('content', '')
            if not isinstance(text, str):
                raise TypeError('assistant content must be a string')

                    continue

                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue

[DEBUG] [PY] code preview:
def tshirt_cheapest_variant_claim_matches_tool_data(trajectory, current_step_index):
    """Check that the assistant's cheapest T-shirt claim matches product data."""
    import json
    import re
    print('tshirt_cheapest_variant_claim_matches_tool_data')
    step = trajectory['steps'][current_step_index]
    assistant_text = ' '.join(sub.get('content', '') for sub in step.get('substeps', []) if sub.get('role') == 'assistant')
    if not isinstance(assistant_text, str):
        raise TypeError('assistant content must be a string')
    if not re.search(r'cheapest.{0,40}t-?shirt|t-?shirt.{0,40}cheapest', assistant_text, re.IGNORECASE):
        print('no cheapest T-shirt claim')
        return True
    if current_step_index == 0:
        return False
    prior_tool_contents = [sub.get('content', '') for sub in trajectory['steps'][current_step_index - 1].get('substeps', []) if sub.get('role') == 'tool']
    if not prior_tool_contents:
        return False
    data = json.loads(prior_tool_contents[-1])
    variants = data.get('variants') if isinstance(data, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(variants, dict):
        raise TypeError('product details must contain a variants object')
…(trunc)
